                           2.0 * (np.dot(a, a) + 2.0 * np.dot(v, b)),
                           6.0 * np.dot(a, b), 4.0 * np.dot(b, b)))
    nonzero = np.flatnonzero(derivative)
    roots = np.polynomial.polynomial.polyroots(derivative[:nonzero[-1] + 1]) if len(nonzero) else ()
    times = [0.0, duration]
    for root in roots:
        if abs(float(root.imag)) <= 1.0e-8 * max(1.0, abs(float(root.real))):
            if 0.0 < float(root.real) < duration:
                times.append(float(root.real))
    return max(float(np.linalg.norm(v + a * t + b * t * t)) for t in times)
def executable_segment_limits(coefficients: Sequence, durations: Sequence[float]) -> dict[str, float]:
    maxima = {name: 0.0 for name in ("speed", "acceleration", "speed_xy", "speed_z", "acceleration_xy", "acceleration_z")}
    for c, duration in zip(np.asarray(coefficients, dtype=np.float64), durations):
        for suffix, axes in (("", (0, 1, 2)), ("_xy", (0, 1)), ("_z", (2,))):
            maxima["speed" + suffix] = max(maxima["speed" + suffix], _maximum_speed(c, float(duration), axes))
            selected = c[list(axes)]
            acc = max(float(np.linalg.norm(2.0 * selected[:, 2] + 6.0 * selected[:, 3] * t)) for t in (0.0, float(duration)))
            maxima["acceleration" + suffix] = max(maxima["acceleration" + suffix], acc)
    return maxima
def executable_segments_clear(
    coefficients: Sequence,
    durations: Sequence[float],
    occupancy: np.ndarray,
    frame: GridFrame3D,
    *,
    cancel_callback: Callable[[], bool] | None = None,
) -> bool:
    curves = np.asarray(coefficients, dtype=np.float64)
    times = np.asarray(durations, dtype=np.float64)
    grid = np.asarray(occupancy)
    if curves.ndim != 3 or curves.shape[1:] != (3, 4) or len(curves) != len(times) or not len(curves):
        return False
    if not np.isfinite(curves).all() or not np.isfinite(times).all() or np.any(times <= 0.0):
        return False
    if grid.shape != (frame.layers, frame.rows, frame.cols):
        return False
    origin = np.array((frame.x_min, frame.y_min, frame.z_min), dtype=np.float64)
    resolution = np.array((frame.resolution_m, frame.resolution_m, frame.resolution_z_m), dtype=np.float64)
    shape = np.array((frame.cols, frame.rows, frame.layers))
    if not np.isfinite(origin).all() or not np.isfinite(resolution).all() or np.any(resolution <= 0.0):
        return False
    upper = origin + resolution * shape
    hull_count = 0
    for c, duration in zip(curves, times):
        endpoints, velocities, _ = sample_executable_segment(c, np.array((0.0, duration)))
        if np.any(endpoints < origin) or np.any(endpoints >= upper):
            return False
        control = np.array((endpoints[0], endpoints[0] + velocities[0] * duration / 3.0,
                            endpoints[1] - velocities[1] * duration / 3.0, endpoints[1]))
        stack = [(control, 0)]
        while stack:
            if cancel_callback is not None and cancel_callback():
                raise RuntimeError("executable trajectory planning cancelled")
            hull, depth = stack.pop()
            hull_count += 1
            if hull_count > _COLLISION_MAX_HULLS:
                return False
            lo, hi = hull.min(axis=0), hull.max(axis=0)
            inside = bool(np.all(lo >= origin) and np.all(hi < upper))
            low_cell = np.maximum(0, np.floor((lo - origin) / resolution - 1.0e-10).astype(int))
            high_cell = np.minimum(shape - 1, np.floor((hi - origin) / resolution + 1.0e-10).astype(int))
            if inside:
                occupied = grid[low_cell[2]:high_cell[2] + 1,
                                low_cell[1]:high_cell[1] + 1,
                                low_cell[0]:high_cell[0] + 1]
                if not np.any(occupied):
                    continue
            if depth >= _COLLISION_MAX_DEPTH:
                return False
            a = (hull[0] + hull[1]) * 0.5
            b = (hull[1] + hull[2]) * 0.5
            d = (hull[2] + hull[3]) * 0.5
            e, f = (a + b) * 0.5, (b + d) * 0.5
            midpoint = (e + f) * 0.5
            stack.append((np.array((midpoint, f, d, hull[3])), depth + 1))
            stack.append((np.array((hull[0], a, e, midpoint)), depth + 1))
    return True
from __future__ import annotations
import struct
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Protocol, Sequence
from protocol import (
    CMD_ABORT,
    CMD_UAV_H_START,
    CMD_UAV_LOAD_START,
    CMD_PING,
    CMD_START,
)
from qubo_model import (
    N_HW,
    Problem,
    build_uav_compact_h_payload,
    build_uav_compact_payload,
    decode_spins,
    dequantize_q9_22,
    uav_compact_payload_fingerprints,
    uav_compact_payload_info,
    uav_logical_problem_fingerprints,
)
REQUIRED_SW_BUILD_ID = 0x44325257
REQUIRED_SW_VERSION = 0x04010351
REQUIRED_PL_HEARTBEAT_FLAG = 0x00004000
REQUIRED_PL_IDENTITY_FLAG = 0x00008000
REQUIRED_PL_ACCESS_FLAG = 0x00000008
REQUIRED_DMA_LOADER_FLAG = 0x00040000
REQUIRED_UAV_COMPACT_FLAG = 0x00200000
REQUIRED_ACTIVE_N_CORE_FLAG = 0x00400000
START_FLAG_ADAPTIVE_BUDGET = 0x01
START_FLAG_EARLY_RESTART_STOP = 0x02
SEED_STEP = 0x9E3779B9
SOLVE_TIMEOUT_S = 8.0
_NO_PROBLEM_TX_LOAD_MODES = {"UAV-CACHE", "ACTIVE-N-CACHE", "MOCK-CACHE"}
def _problem_tx_rtt_ms(load_mode: str, serial_rtt_ms: float) -> float:
    return 0.0 if str(load_mode).upper() in _NO_PROBLEM_TX_LOAD_MODES else float(serial_rtt_ms)
class HostTransactor(Protocol):
    _fpga_problem_crc: int | None
    _fpga_j_crc: int | None
    _fpga_uav_problem_crc: int | None
    _fpga_uav_j_crc: int | None
    def transact(
        self, cmd: int, payload: bytes = b"", timeout: float = 3.0
    ) -> tuple[bytes, float, str]: ...
    def _decode_ping_payload(self, payload: bytes) -> dict: ...
    def _invalidate_fpga_cache(self) -> None: ...
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FpgaRuntimeStatus:
    status: int
    h_writes: int
    j_commits: int
    build_id: int
    version: int
    node_count: int
    serial_rtt_ms: float
    transport: str = ""
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FpgaSolveResult:
    status: int
    energy_raw: int
    energy: float
    node_count: int
    iterations: int
    elapsed_us: int
    spins: tuple[int, ...]
    binary: tuple[int, ...]
    packed_spins: int
    serial_rtt_ms: float
    restarts: int = 1
    best_restart: int = 0
    transport: str = ""
    load_mode: str = "CACHE"
    load_decode_us: int = 0
    load_pl_us: int = 0
    load_total_us: int = 0
    board_load_mode: int = 0
    transport_rx_us: int = 0
    hw_cycles: int = 0
    perf_stats: int = 0
    perf_stats2: int = 0
    profile_pl_solve_us: int = 0
    profile_result_read_us: int = 0
    profile_restart_overhead_us: int = 0
    adaptive_info: int = 0
    reduced_budget: int = 0
    issued_iteration_budget: int = 0
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FpgaBatchResult:
    load_status: FpgaRuntimeStatus
    runs: tuple[FpgaSolveResult, ...]
def _node_count_from_hardware(hardware: int) -> int:
    code = hardware & 0xFF
    return 256 if code == 0 else code
def decode_status_payload(
    payload: bytes, rtt_ms: float, transport: str = ""
) -> FpgaRuntimeStatus:
    if len(payload) < 20:
        raise ValueError(f"状态负载长度错误: {len(payload)}")
    status, counts, build_id, version, hardware = struct.unpack_from("<IIIII", payload)
    return FpgaRuntimeStatus(
        status=status,
        h_writes=counts & 0xFFFF,
        j_commits=(counts >> 16) & 0xFFFF,
        build_id=build_id,
        version=version,
        node_count=_node_count_from_hardware(hardware),
        serial_rtt_ms=rtt_ms,
        transport=transport,
    )
def decode_result_payload(
    payload: bytes,
    rtt_ms: float,
    transport: str = "",
    load_mode: str = "CACHE",
) -> FpgaSolveResult:
    spin_bytes = (N_HW * 2 + 7) // 8
    meta = 8 + spin_bytes
    if len(payload) < meta + 8:
        raise ValueError(f"FPGA结果负载长度错误: {len(payload)}")
    status = struct.unpack_from("<I", payload, 0)[0]
    energy_raw = struct.unpack_from("<i", payload, 4)[0]
    spin_payload = payload[8 : 8 + spin_bytes]
    spins = tuple(decode_spins(spin_payload, N_HW))
    iterations = struct.unpack_from("<H", payload, meta)[0]
    restarts = payload[meta + 2]
    best_restart = payload[meta + 3]
    elapsed_us = struct.unpack_from("<I", payload, meta + 4)[0]
    ext = [0] * 14
    if len(payload) >= meta + 64:
        ext = list(struct.unpack_from("<14I", payload, meta + 8))
    packed = int.from_bytes(spin_payload, "little")
    return FpgaSolveResult(
        status=status,
        energy_raw=energy_raw,
        energy=dequantize_q9_22(energy_raw),
        node_count=N_HW,
        iterations=iterations,
        elapsed_us=elapsed_us,
        spins=spins,
        binary=tuple(1 if spin > 0 else 0 for spin in spins),
        packed_spins=packed,
        serial_rtt_ms=rtt_ms,
        restarts=restarts,
        best_restart=best_restart,
        transport=transport,
        load_mode=load_mode,
        load_decode_us=ext[0],
        load_pl_us=ext[1],
        load_total_us=ext[2],
        board_load_mode=ext[3],
        transport_rx_us=ext[4],
        hw_cycles=ext[5],
        perf_stats=ext[6],
        perf_stats2=ext[7],
        profile_pl_solve_us=ext[8],
        profile_result_read_us=ext[9],
        profile_restart_overhead_us=ext[10],
        adaptive_info=ext[11],
        reduced_budget=ext[12],
        issued_iteration_budget=ext[13],
    )
class FpgaUavSolver:
    def __init__(
        self,
        host: HostTransactor,
        adaptive_budget: bool = False,
        early_restart_stop: bool = False,
    ) -> None:
        self.host = host
        self.adaptive_budget = bool(adaptive_budget)
        self.early_restart_stop = bool(early_restart_stop)
        self._last_runtime: FpgaRuntimeStatus | None = None
        self._auto_seed = 0x12802026
        self._warm_start_notice_sent = False
        self._last_payload_signature: tuple[int, str, int, int] | None = None
        self.last_host_profile: dict[str, float | str | int] = {}
        self._last_tx_wall_ms = 0.0
        self._last_decode_ms = 0.0
        self._last_load_mode = ""
    def _log(self, message: str) -> None:
        signal = getattr(self.host, "log_message", None)
        if signal is not None:
            signal.emit(message)
            return
        append = getattr(self.host, "append", None)
        if append is not None:
            append(message)
    def _runtime_from_ping(
        self, info: dict, rtt_ms: float, transport: str
    ) -> FpgaRuntimeStatus:
        counts = int(info.get("counts", 0))
        return FpgaRuntimeStatus(
            status=int(info.get("status", 0)),
            h_writes=counts & 0xFFFF,
            j_commits=(counts >> 16) & 0xFFFF,
            build_id=int(info.get("build_id", 0)),
            version=int(info.get("version", 0)),
            node_count=_node_count_from_hardware(int(info.get("hardware", 0))),
            serial_rtt_ms=rtt_ms,
            transport=transport,
        )
    def _validate_runtime_info(self, info: dict) -> None:
        if (
            int(info["build_id"]) != REQUIRED_SW_BUILD_ID
            or int(info["version"]) != REQUIRED_SW_VERSION
        ):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "固件版本不匹配，已阻止UAV FPGA规划。"
                f"要求build={REQUIRED_SW_BUILD_ID:08X}, "
                f"version={REQUIRED_SW_VERSION:08X}；"
                f"实际build={int(info['build_id']):08X}, "
                f"version={int(info['version']):08X}。"
            )
        flags = int(info.get("transport_flags", 0))
        missing = []
        if (flags & REQUIRED_PL_HEARTBEAT_FLAG) == 0:
            missing.append("PL_HEARTBEAT")
        if (flags & REQUIRED_PL_IDENTITY_FLAG) == 0:
            missing.append("PL_IDENTITY")
        if (flags & REQUIRED_PL_ACCESS_FLAG) == 0:
            missing.append("PL_ACCESS")
        if (flags & REQUIRED_DMA_LOADER_FLAG) == 0:
            missing.append("DMA_LOADER")
        if (flags & REQUIRED_UAV_COMPACT_FLAG) == 0:
            missing.append("UAV_COMPACT")
        if (flags & REQUIRED_ACTIVE_N_CORE_FLAG) == 0:
            missing.append("ACTIVE_N_CORE")
        if missing:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "PL安全门禁未通过，已阻止UAV LOAD/START："
                + ", ".join(missing)
            )
        hardware = int(info["hardware"])
        reported_n = _node_count_from_hardware(hardware)
        reported_words = (hardware >> 8) & 0xFF
        reported_chunks = (hardware >> 16) & 0xFFFF
        expected_words = (N_HW + 31) // 32
        expected_chunks = N_HW * expected_words
        if (reported_n, reported_words, reported_chunks) != (
            N_HW,
            expected_words,
            expected_chunks,
        ):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "PL规模/存储布局不匹配："
                f"PING报告N={reported_n}, words/row={reported_words}, "
                f"chunks={reported_chunks}；要求N={N_HW}, "
                f"words/row={expected_words}, chunks={expected_chunks}。"
            )
    def preflight_host_path(self) -> None:
        if not self._warm_start_notice_sent:
            self._log(
                "UAV提示：256运行时START协议不接收显式warm-start向量；"
                "当前将warm-start信息折叠到批量multi-start的base seed中。"
            )
            self._warm_start_notice_sent = True
    def ping(self, require_current_runtime: bool = True) -> FpgaRuntimeStatus:
        payload, rtt_ms, transport = self.host.transact(CMD_PING, b"", 5.0)
        info = self.host._decode_ping_payload(payload)
        if require_current_runtime:
            self._validate_runtime_info(info)
        runtime = self._runtime_from_ping(info, rtt_ms, transport)
        self._last_runtime = runtime
        return runtime
    def load(self, problem: Problem) -> FpgaRuntimeStatus:
        problem.validate()
        if self._last_runtime is None:
            self.ping(require_current_runtime=True)
        problem_crc, j_crc = uav_logical_problem_fingerprints(problem)
        payload = build_uav_compact_payload(problem)
        start_payload = self._start_payload(1, 1, self._next_seed_base(None))
        response, rtt_ms, transport = self.host.transact(
            CMD_UAV_LOAD_START, payload + start_payload, timeout=SOLVE_TIMEOUT_S
        )
        result = decode_result_payload(response, rtt_ms, transport, "UAV-COMPACT-LOAD")
        if result.node_count != N_HW or (result.status & 4) == 0:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"FPGA配置失败: status=0x{result.status:08X}, nodes={result.node_count}"
            )
        runtime = self._last_runtime
        status = FpgaRuntimeStatus(
            status=result.status,
            h_writes=runtime.h_writes if runtime else 0,
            j_commits=runtime.j_commits if runtime else 0,
            build_id=runtime.build_id if runtime else REQUIRED_SW_BUILD_ID,
            version=runtime.version if runtime else REQUIRED_SW_VERSION,
            node_count=N_HW,
            serial_rtt_ms=rtt_ms,
            transport=transport,
        )
        self.host._fpga_uav_problem_crc = problem_crc
        self.host._fpga_uav_j_crc = j_crc
        self.host._fpga_problem_crc = None
        self.host._fpga_j_crc = None
        self._last_runtime = status
        return status
    def _next_seed_base(self, seed: int | None) -> int:
        if seed is not None:
            return int(seed) & 0xFFFFFFFF
        value = self._auto_seed & 0xFFFFFFFF
        self._auto_seed = (self._auto_seed + 0x85EBCA6B) & 0xFFFFFFFF
        return value
    @staticmethod
    def _mix_warm_seed(seed: int, warm_start: Sequence[int] | None) -> int:
        if not warm_start:
            return seed & 0xFFFFFFFF
        mixed = seed & 0xFFFFFFFF
        for index, value in enumerate(warm_start[:N_HW]):
            if int(value):
                mixed ^= ((index + 1) * 0x45D9F3B) & 0xFFFFFFFF
                mixed = ((mixed << 13) | (mixed >> 19)) & 0xFFFFFFFF
        return mixed & 0xFFFFFFFF
    def _start_payload(self, iterations: int, restarts: int, seed: int) -> bytes:
        if not 1 <= int(iterations) <= 65535:
            raise ValueError("iterations必须在1..65535")
        if not 1 <= int(restarts) <= 32:
            raise ValueError("restarts必须在1..32")
        flags = START_FLAG_ADAPTIVE_BUDGET if self.adaptive_budget else 0
        if self.early_restart_stop:
            flags |= START_FLAG_EARLY_RESTART_STOP
        return struct.pack(
            "<HBBI",
            int(iterations),
            int(restarts),
            flags,
            int(seed) & 0xFFFFFFFF,
        )
    def _solve_once(
        self,
        full_payload: bytes,
        h_payload: bytes,
        problem_crc: int,
        j_crc: int,
        iterations: int,
        seed: int,
    ) -> FpgaSolveResult:
        start_payload = self._start_payload(iterations, 1, seed)
        cached_problem = getattr(self.host, "_fpga_uav_problem_crc", None)
        cached_j = getattr(self.host, "_fpga_uav_j_crc", None)
        if cached_problem == problem_crc:
            command = CMD_START
            payload = start_payload
            load_mode = "UAV-CACHE"
        elif cached_j == j_crc:
            command = CMD_UAV_H_START
            if h_payload is None:
                raise RuntimeError("internal: H-cache selected without H payload")
            payload = h_payload + start_payload
            load_mode = "UAV-H-CACHE"
        else:
            command = CMD_UAV_LOAD_START
            payload = full_payload + start_payload
            load_mode = "UAV-COMPACT"
        tx_started = time.perf_counter()
        try:
            response, rtt_ms, transport = self.host.transact(
                command, payload, timeout=SOLVE_TIMEOUT_S
            )
        except RuntimeError as exc:
            if command in (CMD_START, CMD_UAV_H_START) and "问题尚未配置" in str(exc):
                self.host._invalidate_fpga_cache()
                response, rtt_ms, transport = self.host.transact(
                    CMD_UAV_LOAD_START, full_payload + start_payload, timeout=SOLVE_TIMEOUT_S
                )
                load_mode = "UAV-COMPACT-RETRY"
            else:
                raise
        self._last_tx_wall_ms = (time.perf_counter() - tx_started) * 1000.0
        decode_started = time.perf_counter()
        result = decode_result_payload(response, rtt_ms, transport, load_mode)
        self._last_decode_ms = (time.perf_counter() - decode_started) * 1000.0
        self._last_load_mode = load_mode
        if result.node_count != N_HW or (result.status & 4) == 0:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"FPGA求解未完成: status=0x{result.status:08X}, nodes={result.node_count}"
            )
        self.host._fpga_uav_problem_crc = problem_crc
        self.host._fpga_uav_j_crc = j_crc
        self.host._fpga_problem_crc = None
        self.host._fpga_j_crc = None
        return result
    def _solve_batch_once(
        self,
        problem: Problem,
        full_payload: bytes | None,
        h_payload: bytes | None,
        problem_crc: int,
        j_crc: int,
        iterations: int,
        restarts: int,
        seed: int,
    ) -> FpgaSolveResult:
        start_payload = self._start_payload(iterations, restarts, seed)
        cached_problem = getattr(self.host, "_fpga_uav_problem_crc", None)
        cached_j = getattr(self.host, "_fpga_uav_j_crc", None)
        if cached_problem == problem_crc:
            command = CMD_START
            payload = start_payload
            load_mode = "UAV-CACHE"
        elif cached_j == j_crc:
            command = CMD_UAV_H_START
            if h_payload is None:
                raise RuntimeError("internal: H-cache selected without H payload")
            payload = h_payload + start_payload
            load_mode = "UAV-H-CACHE"
        else:
            command = CMD_UAV_LOAD_START
            if full_payload is None:
                full_payload = build_uav_compact_payload(problem)
            payload = full_payload + start_payload
            load_mode = "UAV-COMPACT"
        tx_started = time.perf_counter()
        try:
            response, rtt_ms, transport = self.host.transact(
                command, payload, timeout=SOLVE_TIMEOUT_S
            )
        except RuntimeError as exc:
            if command in (CMD_START, CMD_UAV_H_START) and "问题尚未配置" in str(exc):
                self.host._invalidate_fpga_cache()
                if full_payload is None:
                    full_payload = build_uav_compact_payload(problem)
                response, rtt_ms, transport = self.host.transact(
                    CMD_UAV_LOAD_START, full_payload + start_payload, timeout=SOLVE_TIMEOUT_S
                )
                load_mode = "UAV-COMPACT-RETRY"
            else:
                raise
        self._last_tx_wall_ms = (time.perf_counter() - tx_started) * 1000.0
        decode_started = time.perf_counter()
        result = decode_result_payload(response, rtt_ms, transport, load_mode)
        self._last_decode_ms = (time.perf_counter() - decode_started) * 1000.0
        self._last_load_mode = load_mode
        if result.node_count != N_HW or (result.status & 4) == 0:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"FPGA求解未完成: status=0x{result.status:08X}, nodes={result.node_count}"
            )
        self.host._fpga_uav_problem_crc = problem_crc
        self.host._fpga_uav_j_crc = j_crc
        self.host._fpga_problem_crc = None
        self.host._fpga_j_crc = None
        return result
    def start(
        self,
        iterations: int,
        seed: int | None = None,
        warm_start: Sequence[int] | None = None,
    ) -> FpgaSolveResult:
        if getattr(self.host, "_fpga_uav_problem_crc", None) is None:
            raise RuntimeError("当前没有已缓存的 Active-N compact 问题，请先调用 solve_runs/load。")
        run_seed = self._mix_warm_seed(self._next_seed_base(seed), warm_start)
        payload = self._start_payload(iterations, 1, run_seed)
        response, rtt_ms, transport = self.host.transact(
            CMD_START, payload, timeout=SOLVE_TIMEOUT_S
        )
        result = decode_result_payload(response, rtt_ms, transport, "UAV-CACHE")
        if result.node_count != N_HW or (result.status & 4) == 0:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"FPGA求解未完成: status=0x{result.status:08X}, nodes={result.node_count}"
            )
        return result
    def solve_runs(
        self,
        problem: Problem,
        iterations: int,
        restarts: int = 1,
        seed: int | None = None,
        warm_starts: Sequence[Sequence[int]] = (),
    ) -> FpgaBatchResult:
        if restarts < 1:
            raise ValueError("restarts必须为正整数")
        if restarts > 32:
            raise ValueError("restarts不能超过32")
        if self._last_runtime is None:
            self.ping(require_current_runtime=True)
        if warm_starts and not self._warm_start_notice_sent:
            self._log(
                "UAV提示：256运行时START协议不接收显式warm-start向量；"
                "当前将warm-start信息折叠到批量multi-start的base seed中。"
            )
            self._warm_start_notice_sent = True
        host_started = time.perf_counter()
        problem.validate()
        payload_started = time.perf_counter()
        fingerprint_started = time.perf_counter()
        problem_crc, j_crc = uav_logical_problem_fingerprints(problem)
        fingerprint_ms = (time.perf_counter() - fingerprint_started) * 1000.0
        cached_problem = getattr(self.host, "_fpga_uav_problem_crc", None)
        cached_j = getattr(self.host, "_fpga_uav_j_crc", None)
        full_payload = None
        h_payload = None
        payload_build_mode = "CACHE"
        payload_prepared_bytes = 0
        if cached_problem == problem_crc:
            payload_build_mode = "CACHE"
        elif cached_j == j_crc:
            payload_build_mode = "H"
            h_payload = build_uav_compact_h_payload(problem)
            payload_prepared_bytes = len(h_payload)
        else:
            payload_build_mode = "FULL"
            full_payload = build_uav_compact_payload(problem)
            payload_prepared_bytes = len(full_payload)
        payload_ms = (time.perf_counter() - payload_started) * 1000.0
        if full_payload is not None:
            info = uav_compact_payload_info(full_payload)
            signature = (
                int(info.get("active_n") or 0),
                str(info.get("format") or ""),
                int(info.get("bytes") or 0),
                int(info.get("shift") or 0),
            )
            self.host._fpga_uav_payload_bytes = int(signature[2])
            if signature != self._last_payload_signature:
                self._log(
                    "UAV FPGA Active-N payload "
                    f"n={signature[0]} format={signature[1]} "
                    f"bytes={signature[2]} shift={signature[3]}"
                )
                self._last_payload_signature = signature
        reported_payload_bytes = int(
            len(full_payload) if full_payload is not None
            else getattr(self.host, "_fpga_uav_payload_bytes", payload_prepared_bytes) or payload_prepared_bytes
        )
        base_seed = self._next_seed_base(seed)
        if warm_starts:
            base_seed = self._mix_warm_seed(base_seed, warm_starts[0])
        batch_started = time.perf_counter()
        result = self._solve_batch_once(
            problem,
            full_payload,
            h_payload,
            problem_crc,
            j_crc,
            int(iterations),
            int(restarts),
            base_seed,
        )
        batch_ms = (time.perf_counter() - batch_started) * 1000.0
        load_rtt = _problem_tx_rtt_ms(result.load_mode, result.serial_rtt_ms)
        runtime = self._last_runtime
        load_status = FpgaRuntimeStatus(
            status=result.status,
            h_writes=runtime.h_writes if runtime else 0,
            j_commits=runtime.j_commits if runtime else 0,
            build_id=runtime.build_id if runtime else REQUIRED_SW_BUILD_ID,
            version=runtime.version if runtime else REQUIRED_SW_VERSION,
            node_count=N_HW,
            serial_rtt_ms=load_rtt,
            transport=result.transport,
        )
        self.last_host_profile = {
            "payload_ms": float(payload_ms),
            "fingerprint_ms": float(fingerprint_ms),
            "payload_build_mode": str(payload_build_mode),
            "payload_prepared_bytes": int(payload_prepared_bytes),
            "payload_cache_fastpath": bool(payload_build_mode in {"CACHE", "H"}),
            "batch_ms": float(batch_ms),
            "transport_wall_ms": float(self._last_tx_wall_ms),
            "decode_ms": float(self._last_decode_ms),
            "host_total_ms": float((time.perf_counter() - host_started) * 1000.0),
            "wire_rtt_ms": float(result.serial_rtt_ms),
            "load_mode": str(result.load_mode),
            "payload_bytes": int(reported_payload_bytes),
            "native_wire_payload": bool(getattr(problem, "_native_wire_payload", False)),
            "native_wire_payload_version": int(getattr(problem, "_native_wire_payload_version", 0) or 0),
        }
        return FpgaBatchResult(load_status, (result,))
    def abort(self) -> FpgaRuntimeStatus:
        payload, rtt_ms, transport = self.host.transact(CMD_ABORT, b"", 2.0)
        return decode_status_payload(payload, rtt_ms, transport)
FpgaSolver = FpgaUavSolver
from __future__ import annotations
import ctypes
import ctypes.util
import os
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Sequence
import numpy as np
_ENABLED = os.environ.get("FPGA_NATIVE_FASTPATH", "auto").strip().lower() not in {"0", "false", "off", "no"}
_LIB = None
_LOAD_ERROR = None
_PATH_CACHE: dict[int, np.ndarray] = {}
_REQUIRED_ABI = 3301
def set_enabled(value: bool) -> None:
    global _ENABLED
    _ENABLED = bool(value)
def _candidate_library_paths() -> list[Path]:
    here = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    names = ["libfpga_fastpath_native.so"]
    out: list[Path] = []
    explicit = os.environ.get("FPGA_FASTPATH_LIB", "").strip()
    if explicit:
        out.append(Path(explicit).expanduser())
    for name in names:
        out.append(here / "_native" / name)
        out.append(here / name)
    for item in os.environ.get("LD_LIBRARY_PATH", "").split(":"):
        if item:
            out.append(Path(item) / names[0])
    for parent in here.parents:
        out.append(parent / "devel" / "lib" / names[0])
        out.append(parent / "install" / "lib" / names[0])
    found = ctypes.util.find_library("fpga_fastpath_native")
    if found:
        out.append(Path(found))
    seen = set()
    unique = []
    for p in out:
        key = str(p)
        if key not in seen:
            seen.add(key)
            unique.append(p)
    return unique
def _configure(lib) -> None:
    c_void_p = ctypes.c_void_p
    c_i = ctypes.c_int
    c_i64 = ctypes.c_int64
    c_d = ctypes.c_double
    lib.fpga_native_fastpath_version.argtypes = []
    lib.fpga_native_fastpath_version.restype = c_i
    lib.fpga_udp_recv_native.argtypes = [
        c_i, c_void_p, c_i, c_i, c_i, c_i, c_void_p, c_void_p, c_void_p,
    ]
    lib.fpga_udp_recv_native.restype = c_i
    lib.fpga_voxelize_inflate3d.argtypes = [
        c_void_p, c_i64, c_i, c_i, c_i,
        c_d, c_d, c_d, c_d, c_d, c_d, c_void_p, c_void_p,
    ]
    lib.fpga_voxelize_inflate3d.restype = c_i
    lib.fpga_clearance_limited3d.argtypes = [
        c_void_p, c_i, c_i, c_i, c_i, c_void_p,
    ]
    lib.fpga_clearance_limited3d.restype = c_i
    lib.fpga_chain_blocked_count_xyz3d.argtypes = [
        c_void_p, c_i, c_void_p, c_i, c_i, c_i,
        c_d, c_d, c_d, c_d, c_d, c_i,
    ]
    lib.fpga_chain_blocked_count_xyz3d.restype = c_i
    lib.fpga_candidates3d.argtypes = [
        c_void_p, c_i, c_void_p, c_void_p, c_i, c_i, c_i,
        c_d, c_d, c_d, c_d, c_d, c_i, c_i, c_d, c_void_p,
        c_void_p, c_void_p, c_void_p, c_void_p, c_void_p,
    ]
    lib.fpga_candidates3d.restype = c_i
    lib.fpga_astar26.argtypes = [
        c_void_p, c_void_p, c_i, c_i, c_i,
        c_i, c_i, c_i, c_i, c_i, c_i, c_void_p, c_i,
    ]
    lib.fpga_astar26.restype = c_i
    lib.fpga_blocked_pairs_output_size.argtypes = [c_void_p, c_i]
    lib.fpga_blocked_pairs_output_size.restype = c_i64
    lib.fpga_blocked_pairs3d.argtypes = [
        c_void_p, c_void_p, c_i, c_void_p, c_i, c_i, c_i, c_i, c_void_p, c_i64,
    ]
    lib.fpga_blocked_pairs3d.restype = c_i64
    lib.fpga_segments_blocked_xyz3d.argtypes = [
        c_void_p, c_void_p, c_i, c_void_p, c_i, c_i, c_i,
        c_d, c_d, c_d, c_d, c_d, c_i, c_void_p,
    ]
    lib.fpga_segments_blocked_xyz3d.restype = c_i
    lib.fpga_segments_blocked_cells3d.argtypes = [
        c_void_p, c_void_p, c_i, c_void_p, c_i, c_i, c_i, c_void_p,
    ]
    lib.fpga_segments_blocked_cells3d.restype = c_i
    lib.fpga_dense_trajectory_output_size.argtypes = [c_i, c_d, c_d]
    lib.fpga_dense_trajectory_output_size.restype = c_i64
    lib.fpga_dense_trajectory3d.argtypes = [
        c_void_p, c_void_p, c_i, c_d, c_d, c_d, c_i,
        c_void_p, c_void_p, c_void_p, c_i64, c_void_p,
    ]
    lib.fpga_dense_trajectory3d.restype = c_i
    lib.fpga_uav_compact_payload_active.argtypes = [
        c_void_p, c_void_p, c_i, c_i, c_void_p, c_i,
    ]
    lib.fpga_uav_compact_payload_active.restype = c_i
    lib.fpga_kino_qubo_active.argtypes = [
        c_void_p, c_i, c_void_p, c_void_p, c_void_p, c_i,
        c_void_p, c_void_p, c_void_p,
        c_void_p, c_void_p, c_void_p, c_void_p,
        c_d, c_d, c_d, c_d, c_d, c_d, c_d, c_d, c_d, c_d, c_d,
        c_void_p, c_void_p, c_void_p, c_void_p, c_void_p, c_void_p,
    ]
    lib.fpga_kino_qubo_active.restype = c_i
def _load():
    global _LIB, _LOAD_ERROR
    if _LIB is not None:
        return _LIB
    if _LOAD_ERROR is not None:
        return None
    errors = []
    for path in _candidate_library_paths():
        try:
            if path.is_absolute() and not path.exists():
                continue
            lib = ctypes.CDLL(str(path))
            _configure(lib)
            if int(lib.fpga_native_fastpath_version()) < _REQUIRED_ABI:
                raise RuntimeError(f"native fastpath ABI is too old; require >= {_REQUIRED_ABI}")
            _LIB = lib
            return _LIB
        except Exception as exc:
            errors.append(f"{path}: {exc}")
    _LOAD_ERROR = "; ".join(errors) if errors else "libfpga_fastpath_native.so not found"
    return None
def available() -> bool:
    return bool(_ENABLED and _load() is not None)
def status() -> dict[str, object]:
    lib = _load() if _ENABLED else None
    return {
        "enabled": bool(_ENABLED),
        "available": bool(lib is not None),
        "version": int(lib.fpga_native_fastpath_version()) if lib is not None else 0,
        "error": _LOAD_ERROR,
    }
def _ptr(array: np.ndarray | None):
    if array is None:
        return None
    address = int(array.__array_interface__["data"][0])
    return ctypes.c_void_p(address)
def voxelize_inflate3d(points_xyz, frame, radius_m: float):
    lib = _load() if _ENABLED else None
    if lib is None:
        return None
    points = np.ascontiguousarray(points_xyz, dtype=np.float64)
    if points.ndim != 2 or points.shape[1] != 3:
        return None
    raw = np.zeros((frame.layers, frame.rows, frame.cols), dtype=np.uint8)
    inflated = np.zeros_like(raw)
    rc = lib.fpga_voxelize_inflate3d(
        _ptr(points), int(points.shape[0]),
        int(frame.layers), int(frame.rows), int(frame.cols),
        float(frame.x_min), float(frame.y_min), float(frame.z_min),
        float(frame.resolution_m), float(frame.resolution_z_m), float(radius_m),
        _ptr(raw), _ptr(inflated),
    )
    if rc != 0:
        return None
    return raw, inflated
def clearance_limited3d(occupancy: np.ndarray, max_distance_voxels: float):
    lib = _load() if _ENABLED else None
    if lib is None or not hasattr(lib, "fpga_clearance_limited3d"):
        return None
    occ = np.ascontiguousarray(occupancy, dtype=np.uint8)
    if occ.ndim != 3:
        return None
    cap = max(1, int(np.ceil(max(0.0, float(max_distance_voxels)))))
    out = np.empty(occ.shape, dtype=np.float32)
    rc = int(lib.fpga_clearance_limited3d(_ptr(occ), *map(int, occ.shape), cap, _ptr(out)))
    return out if rc == 0 else None
def chain_blocked_count_xyz3d(points_xyz, occupancy: np.ndarray, frame, samples: int = 8):
    lib = _load() if _ENABLED else None
    if lib is None or not hasattr(lib, "fpga_chain_blocked_count_xyz3d"):
        return None
    points = np.ascontiguousarray(points_xyz, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1, 3)
    if points.shape[0] < 2:
        return 0
    occ = np.ascontiguousarray(occupancy, dtype=np.uint8)
    if occ.ndim != 3:
        return None
    rc = int(lib.fpga_chain_blocked_count_xyz3d(
        _ptr(points), int(points.shape[0]), _ptr(occ), *map(int, occ.shape),
        float(frame.x_min), float(frame.y_min), float(frame.z_min),
        float(frame.resolution_m), float(frame.resolution_z_m), max(2, int(samples)),
    ))
    return None if rc < 0 else rc
def candidate_layers3d(
    spine_points_xyz,
    occupancy: np.ndarray,
    clearance: np.ndarray,
    frame,
    *,
    radius: int,
    max_count: int,
    safe_clearance_voxels: float,
    goal_bias=None,
):
    lib = _load() if _ENABLED else None
    if lib is None or int(lib.fpga_native_fastpath_version()) < _REQUIRED_ABI:
        return None
    spine = np.ascontiguousarray(spine_points_xyz, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1, 3)
    if spine.shape[0] <= 0:
        return ([], [], [], [])
    occ = np.ascontiguousarray(occupancy, dtype=np.uint8)
    clr = np.ascontiguousarray(clearance, dtype=np.float32)
    if occ.ndim != 3 or clr.shape != occ.shape:
        return None
    radius = max(1, int(radius))
    max_count = max(1, int(max_count))
    bias = None if goal_bias is None else np.ascontiguousarray(goal_bias, dtype=np.float64).reshape(3)
    counts = np.zeros(spine.shape[0], dtype=np.int32)
    cells = np.empty((spine.shape[0], max_count, 3), dtype=np.int32)
    positions = np.empty((spine.shape[0], max_count, 3), dtype=np.float64)
    obstacle = np.empty((spine.shape[0], max_count), dtype=np.float64)
    reference = np.empty((spine.shape[0], max_count), dtype=np.float64)
    rc = int(lib.fpga_candidates3d(
        _ptr(spine), int(spine.shape[0]), _ptr(occ), _ptr(clr), *map(int, occ.shape),
        float(frame.x_min), float(frame.y_min), float(frame.z_min),
        float(frame.resolution_m), float(frame.resolution_z_m),
        radius, max_count, float(safe_clearance_voxels), _ptr(bias),
        _ptr(counts), _ptr(cells), _ptr(positions), _ptr(obstacle), _ptr(reference),
    ))
    if rc != spine.shape[0]:
        return None
    out_positions = []
    out_cells = []
    out_obstacle = []
    out_reference = []
    for layer, count in enumerate(counts.tolist()):
        count = max(0, min(int(count), max_count))
        out_cells.append([
            (int(v[0]), int(v[1]), int(v[2])) for v in cells[layer, :count]
        ])
        out_positions.append([
            (float(v[0]), float(v[1]), float(v[2])) for v in positions[layer, :count]
        ])
        out_obstacle.append([float(v) for v in obstacle[layer, :count]])
        out_reference.append([float(v) for v in reference[layer, :count]])
    return out_positions, out_cells, out_obstacle, out_reference
def astar26(occupancy: np.ndarray, start, goal, bias: np.ndarray | None = None):
    lib = _load() if _ENABLED else None
    if lib is None:
        return None
    occ = np.ascontiguousarray(occupancy, dtype=np.uint8)
    if occ.ndim != 3:
        return None
    bias_arr = None if bias is None else np.ascontiguousarray(bias, dtype=np.float64)
    if bias_arr is not None and bias_arr.shape != occ.shape:
        return None
    cap = int(occ.size)
    buf = _PATH_CACHE.get(cap)
    if buf is None:
        buf = np.empty((cap, 3), dtype=np.int32)
        if len(_PATH_CACHE) > 8:
            _PATH_CACHE.clear()
        _PATH_CACHE[cap] = buf
    n = lib.fpga_astar26(
        _ptr(occ), _ptr(bias_arr), *map(int, occ.shape),
        int(start[0]), int(start[1]), int(start[2]),
        int(goal[0]), int(goal[1]), int(goal[2]),
        _ptr(buf), cap,
    )
    if n < 0:
        return None
    if n == 0:
        return ()
    return tuple((int(z), int(r), int(c)) for z, r, c in buf[:n])
def blocked_pairs3d(candidate_cells: Sequence[Sequence[Sequence[int]]], occupancy: np.ndarray, samples: int = 8):
    lib = _load() if _ENABLED else None
    if lib is None or len(candidate_cells) < 2:
        return None
    counts = [len(layer) for layer in candidate_cells]
    offsets = np.zeros(len(counts) + 1, dtype=np.int32)
    offsets[1:] = np.cumsum(counts, dtype=np.int32)
    if int(offsets[-1]) <= 0:
        return None
    flat = np.ascontiguousarray(
        np.asarray([cell for layer in candidate_cells for cell in layer], dtype=np.int32).reshape(-1, 3)
    )
    occ = np.ascontiguousarray(occupancy, dtype=np.uint8)
    required = int(lib.fpga_blocked_pairs_output_size(_ptr(offsets), len(counts)))
    if required < 0:
        return None
    out = np.empty(required, dtype=np.uint8)
    rc = int(lib.fpga_blocked_pairs3d(
        _ptr(flat), _ptr(offsets), len(counts), _ptr(occ), *map(int, occ.shape),
        max(2, int(samples)), _ptr(out), required,
    ))
    if rc != required:
        return None
    result = []
    pos = 0
    for m, n in zip(counts[:-1], counts[1:]):
        block = out[pos:pos + m*n].reshape(m, n)
        result.append([[bool(v) for v in row] for row in block])
        pos += m*n
    return result
def segments_blocked_cells3d(a_zyx, b_zyx, occupancy: np.ndarray):
    lib = _load() if _ENABLED else None
    if lib is None or int(lib.fpga_native_fastpath_version()) < 3101 or not hasattr(lib, "fpga_segments_blocked_cells3d"):
        return None
    a = np.ascontiguousarray(a_zyx, dtype=np.int32).reshape(-1, 3)
    b = np.ascontiguousarray(b_zyx, dtype=np.int32).reshape(-1, 3)
    if a.shape != b.shape:
        return None
    occ = np.ascontiguousarray(occupancy, dtype=np.uint8)
    if occ.ndim != 3:
        return None
    out = np.empty(a.shape[0], dtype=np.uint8)
    rc = int(lib.fpga_segments_blocked_cells3d(
        _ptr(a), _ptr(b), int(a.shape[0]), _ptr(occ), *map(int, occ.shape), _ptr(out),
    ))
    if rc != a.shape[0]:
        return None
    return [bool(v) for v in out]
def dense_trajectory3d(points_xyz, velocities_xyz, dt: float, dt_sample: float, max_vel: float, *, linear_only: bool = False):
    lib = _load() if _ENABLED else None
    if lib is None or int(lib.fpga_native_fastpath_version()) < 3201 or not hasattr(lib, "fpga_dense_trajectory3d"):
        return None
    points = np.ascontiguousarray(np.asarray(points_xyz, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1, 3))
    vel = np.ascontiguousarray(np.asarray(velocities_xyz, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1, 3))
    if points.shape != vel.shape or points.shape[0] <= 0:
        return None
    count = int(lib.fpga_dense_trajectory_output_size(int(points.shape[0]), float(dt), float(dt_sample)))
    if count <= 0 or count > 5_000_000:
        return None
    dense = np.empty((count, 3), dtype=np.float64)
    dense_vel = np.empty((count, 3), dtype=np.float64)
    dense_acc = np.empty((count, 3), dtype=np.float64)
    vmax = ctypes.c_double(0.0)
    rc = int(lib.fpga_dense_trajectory3d(
        _ptr(points), _ptr(vel), int(points.shape[0]), float(dt), float(dt_sample), float(max_vel), int(bool(linear_only)),
        _ptr(dense), _ptr(dense_vel), _ptr(dense_acc), int(count), ctypes.byref(vmax),
    ))
    if rc != count:
        return None
    return dense, dense_vel, dense_acc, float(vmax.value)
def segments_blocked_xyz3d(a_xyz, b_xyz, occupancy: np.ndarray, frame, samples: int = 8):
    lib = _load() if _ENABLED else None
    if lib is None:
        return None
    a = np.ascontiguousarray(a_xyz, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1, 3)
    b = np.ascontiguousarray(b_xyz, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1, 3)
    if a.shape != b.shape:
        return None
    occ = np.ascontiguousarray(occupancy, dtype=np.uint8)
    out = np.empty(a.shape[0], dtype=np.uint8)
    rc = int(lib.fpga_segments_blocked_xyz3d(
        _ptr(a), _ptr(b), int(a.shape[0]), _ptr(occ), *map(int, occ.shape),
        float(frame.x_min), float(frame.y_min), float(frame.z_min),
        float(frame.resolution_m), float(frame.resolution_z_m), max(2, int(samples)), _ptr(out),
    ))
    if rc != a.shape[0]:
        return None
    return [bool(v) for v in out]
def udp_recv_native(fd: int, capacity: int, spin_us: int, poll_us: int, deadline_us: int):
    lib = _load() if _ENABLED else None
    if lib is None or int(lib.fpga_native_fastpath_version()) < _REQUIRED_ABI:
        return None
    capacity = max(256, min(int(capacity), 65536))
    buf = ctypes.create_string_buffer(capacity)
    phase = ctypes.c_int(0)
    elapsed = ctypes.c_int(0)
    spin_elapsed = ctypes.c_int(0)
    rc = int(lib.fpga_udp_recv_native(
        int(fd), ctypes.cast(buf, ctypes.c_void_p), capacity,
        max(0, int(spin_us)), max(50, int(poll_us)), max(1, int(deadline_us)),
        ctypes.byref(phase), ctypes.byref(elapsed), ctypes.byref(spin_elapsed),
    ))
    if rc == 0:
        return None
    if rc < 0:
        raise OSError(-rc, f"native UDP receive failed rc={rc}")
    return (
        bytes(buf.raw[:rc]),
        "spin" if phase.value == 1 else "blocking",
        int(elapsed.value),
        int(spin_elapsed.value),
    )
def uav_compact_payload_active(h, j, n: int, *, h_only: bool = False):
    lib = _load() if _ENABLED else None
    if lib is None or int(lib.fpga_native_fastpath_version()) < 3001 or not hasattr(lib, "fpga_uav_compact_payload_active"):
        return None
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2 or n > 256:
        return None
    ha = np.ascontiguousarray(np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)[:n])
    ja = np.ascontiguousarray(np.asarray(j, dtype=np.float64)[:n, :n])
    if ha.size != n or ja.shape != (n, n):
        return None
    upper = n * (n - 1) // 2
    cap = 4 + 4 * n if h_only else 4 + 4 * n + 4 * upper
    out = np.empty(cap, dtype=np.uint8)
    rc = int(lib.fpga_uav_compact_payload_active(
        _ptr(ha), _ptr(ja), n, int(bool(h_only)), _ptr(out), int(out.size)
    ))
    if rc < 0 or rc > out.size:
        return None
    return out[:rc].tobytes()
def kino_qubo_active(
    *,
    fixed_positions,
    candidate_positions,
    candidate_obstacle_costs,
    candidate_reference_costs,
    candidate_blocked_pairs,
    candidate_endpoint_blocked,
    dt: float,
    w_vel: float,
    w_acc: float,
    w_obs: float,
    w_spine: float,
    w_len: float,
    w_start_vel: float,
    w_start_acc: float,
    w_end_vel: float,
    segment_penalty: float,
    start_vel_xyz,
    start_acc_xyz,
    a_mult: float,
):
    lib = _load() if _ENABLED else None
    if lib is None or int(lib.fpga_native_fastpath_version()) < 3001 or not hasattr(lib, 'fpga_kino_qubo_active'):
        return None
    free_layers = len(candidate_positions)
    if free_layers < 1:
        return None
    counts = np.asarray([len(layer) for layer in candidate_positions], dtype=np.int32)
    offsets = np.zeros(free_layers + 1, dtype=np.int32)
    offsets[1:] = np.cumsum(counts, dtype=np.int32)
    n = int(offsets[-1])
    if n <= 0 or n > 256:
        return None
    candidates = np.ascontiguousarray(
        np.asarray([p for layer in candidate_positions for p in layer], dtype=np.float64).reshape(n, 3)
    )
    obstacle = np.ascontiguousarray(
        np.asarray([v for layer in candidate_obstacle_costs for v in layer], dtype=np.float64).reshape(n)
    )
    has_reference = candidate_reference_costs is not None and float(w_spine) > 0.0
    reference = None
    if has_reference:
        reference = np.ascontiguousarray(
            np.asarray([v for layer in candidate_reference_costs for v in layer], dtype=np.float64).reshape(n)
        )
    pair_total = int(sum(int(counts[i]) * int(counts[i+1]) for i in range(max(0, free_layers - 1))))
    blocked = np.zeros(pair_total, dtype=np.uint8)
    pos = 0
    for li in range(free_layers - 1):
        a, b = int(counts[li]), int(counts[li+1])
        block = np.asarray(candidate_blocked_pairs[li], dtype=np.uint8)[:a, :b].reshape(-1)
        blocked[pos:pos + block.size] = block
        pos += block.size
    first = np.zeros(int(counts[0]), dtype=np.uint8)
    last = np.zeros(int(counts[-1]), dtype=np.uint8)
    if candidate_endpoint_blocked is not None:
        f, l = candidate_endpoint_blocked
        first[:min(first.size, len(f))] = np.asarray(f[:first.size], dtype=np.uint8)
        last[:min(last.size, len(l))] = np.asarray(l[:last.size], dtype=np.uint8)
    start = np.ascontiguousarray(fixed_positions[0], dtype=np.float64).reshape(3)
    goal = np.ascontiguousarray(fixed_positions[-1], dtype=np.float64).reshape(3)
    start_vel = np.ascontiguousarray(start_vel_xyz, dtype=np.float64).reshape(3)
    start_acc = np.ascontiguousarray(start_acc_xyz, dtype=np.float64).reshape(3)
    linear = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    matrix = np.empty((n, n), dtype=np.float64)
    h = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    j = np.empty((n, n), dtype=np.float64)
    info = np.empty(4, dtype=np.float64)
    hashes = np.empty(2, dtype=np.uint64)
    rc = int(lib.fpga_kino_qubo_active(
        _ptr(offsets), free_layers, _ptr(candidates), _ptr(obstacle), _ptr(reference), int(bool(has_reference)),
        _ptr(blocked), _ptr(first), _ptr(last), _ptr(start), _ptr(goal), _ptr(start_vel), _ptr(start_acc),
        float(dt), float(w_vel), float(w_acc), float(w_obs), float(w_spine), float(w_len),
        float(w_start_vel), float(w_start_acc), float(w_end_vel), float(segment_penalty), float(a_mult),
        _ptr(linear), _ptr(matrix), _ptr(h), _ptr(j), _ptr(info), _ptr(hashes),
    ))
    if rc != n:
        return None
    return {
        'n': n,
        'linear': linear,
        'matrix': matrix,
        'h': h,
        'j': j,
        'objective_scale': float(info[0]),
        'one_hot_penalty': float(info[1]),
        'segment_penalty_used': float(info[2]),
        'bfp_rescale': float(info[3]),
        'native_abi': int(lib.fpga_native_fastpath_version()),
        'logical_problem_fingerprints': (int(hashes[0]), int(hashes[1])),
    }
from __future__ import annotations
"""Unified software optimizer bridge for the AC7020C host.
Available software optimizers:
- Kaiwu simulated annealing (SA only)
- Local simulated annealing
- Local tabu search
- Multi-start greedy local search
All results are re-evaluated with the original problem objective before
ranking, so FPGA and software values use the same convention.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
import math
import random
from time import perf_counter
from typing import Any, Dict, List
import numpy as np
from qubo_model import (
    N_HW,
    Problem,
    ising_energy,
    maxcut_value,
    qubo_value,
)
@dataclass
class SoftwareResult:
    model: str
    objective: float
    spins: List[int]
    binary: List[int]
    solver_runtime_s: float
    total_runtime_s: float
    candidate_count: int
    details: Dict[str, Any]
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return asdict(self)
def _objective(problem: Problem, spins: List[int]) -> float:
    padded = list(spins) + [-1] * (N_HW - len(spins))
    if problem.kind == "maxcut":
        return float(maxcut_value(problem, padded))
    if problem.kind == "qubo":
        return float(qubo_value(problem, padded))
    return float(ising_energy(problem, padded, original=True))
def _maximize(problem: Problem) -> bool:
    return problem.kind == "maxcut"
def _better(problem: Problem, lhs: float, rhs: float, eps: float = 1e-12) -> bool:
    if _maximize(problem):
        return lhs > rhs + eps
    return lhs < rhs - eps
def _improvement(problem: Problem, delta: float) -> float:
    return delta if _maximize(problem) else -delta
def _random_spins(rng: random.Random, n: int) -> List[int]:
    return [1 if rng.getrandbits(1) else -1 for _ in range(n)]
def _flip_delta(problem: Problem, spins: List[int], index: int) -> float:
    n = problem.n
    if problem.kind == "maxcut":
        matrix = problem.source_matrix
        spin_i = spins[index]
        delta = 0.0
        for j in range(n):
            if j == index:
                continue
            weight = float(matrix[index][j])
            delta += weight if spin_i == spins[j] else -weight
        return delta
    if problem.kind == "qubo":
        matrix = problem.source_matrix
        binary = [1 if spin > 0 else 0 for spin in spins]
        x_i = binary[index]
        linear = float(matrix[index][index])
        for j in range(n):
            if j == index:
                continue
            linear += (
                float(matrix[index][j]) + float(matrix[j][index])
            ) * binary[j]
        return float((1 - 2 * x_i) * linear)
    spin_i = spins[index]
    interaction = 0.0
    for j in range(n):
        if j != index:
            interaction += float(problem.j[index][j]) * spins[j]
    delta_scaled = (
        2.0 * float(problem.h[index]) * spin_i
        + 4.0 * spin_i * interaction
    )
    scale = float(problem.fpga_scale) if problem.fpga_scale else 1.0
    return delta_scaled / scale
def _binary(spins: List[int]) -> List[int]:
    return [1 if spin > 0 else 0 for spin in spins]
def _result(
    model: str,
    problem: Problem,
    best_spins: List[int],
    runtime_s: float,
    candidate_count: int,
    details: Dict[str, Any],
) -> SoftwareResult:
    objective = _objective(problem, best_spins)
    return SoftwareResult(
        model=model,
        objective=float(objective),
        spins=list(best_spins),
        binary=_binary(best_spins),
        solver_runtime_s=float(runtime_s),
        total_runtime_s=float(runtime_s),
        candidate_count=int(candidate_count),
        details=details,
    )
def solve_local_sa(
    problem: Problem,
    iterations: int,
    restarts: int,
    seed: int,
) -> SoftwareResult:
    start_time = perf_counter()
    iterations = max(1, int(iterations))
    restarts = max(1, int(restarts))
    global_best_spins: List[int] | None = None
    global_best_value: float | None = None
    best_restart = 0
    for restart in range(restarts):
        rng = random.Random((int(seed) + 0x9E3779B9 * restart) & 0xFFFFFFFF)
        spins = _random_spins(rng, problem.n)
        current = _objective(problem, spins)
        local_best_spins = list(spins)
        local_best_value = current
        samples = [
            abs(_flip_delta(problem, spins, rng.randrange(problem.n)))
            for _ in range(min(64, max(8, problem.n)))
        ]
        nonzero = [value for value in samples if value > 1e-12]
        scale = float(np.median(nonzero)) if nonzero else 1.0
        temperature_start = max(scale * 4.0, 1e-6)
        temperature_end = max(temperature_start * 1e-3, 1e-9)
        cooling = (
            (temperature_end / temperature_start) ** (1.0 / max(1, iterations - 1))
            if iterations > 1
            else 1.0
        )
        temperature = temperature_start
        for _step in range(iterations):
            index = rng.randrange(problem.n)
            delta = _flip_delta(problem, spins, index)
            gain = _improvement(problem, delta)
            accept = gain >= 0.0
            if not accept:
                probability = math.exp(max(-700.0, gain / max(temperature, 1e-12)))
                accept = rng.random() < probability
            if accept:
                spins[index] = -spins[index]
                current += delta
                if (_step & 255) == 255:
                    current = _objective(problem, spins)
                if _better(problem, current, local_best_value):
                    local_best_value = current
                    local_best_spins = list(spins)
            temperature *= cooling
        exact_local = _objective(problem, local_best_spins)
        if global_best_value is None or _better(problem, exact_local, global_best_value):
            global_best_value = exact_local
            global_best_spins = local_best_spins
            best_restart = restart
    assert global_best_spins is not None
    runtime = perf_counter() - start_time
    return _result(
        "local_sa",
        problem,
        global_best_spins,
        runtime,
        restarts,
        {
            "backend": "BuiltInSimulatedAnnealing",
            "iterations": iterations,
            "restarts": restarts,
            "best_restart": best_restart,
            "seed": int(seed) & 0xFFFFFFFF,
        },
    )
def solve_tabu(
    problem: Problem,
    iterations: int,
    restarts: int,
    seed: int,
) -> SoftwareResult:
    start_time = perf_counter()
    iterations = max(1, int(iterations))
    restarts = max(1, int(restarts))
    tenure_base = max(5, int(round(math.sqrt(problem.n))))
    global_best_spins: List[int] | None = None
    global_best_value: float | None = None
    best_restart = 0
    for restart in range(restarts):
        rng = random.Random((int(seed) ^ 0xA5A5A5A5 ^ (restart * 7919)) & 0xFFFFFFFF)
        spins = _random_spins(rng, problem.n)
        current = _objective(problem, spins)
        restart_best_spins = list(spins)
        restart_best_value = current
        tabu_until = [0] * problem.n
        for step in range(iterations):
            selected_index = -1
            selected_value: float | None = None
            selected_delta = 0.0
            for index in range(problem.n):
                delta = _flip_delta(problem, spins, index)
                candidate_value = current + delta
                aspiration = (
                    global_best_value is None
                    or _better(problem, candidate_value, global_best_value)
                )
                if step < tabu_until[index] and not aspiration:
                    continue
                if (
                    selected_value is None
                    or _better(problem, candidate_value, selected_value)
                    or (
                        abs(candidate_value - selected_value) <= 1e-12
                        and rng.random() < 0.5
                    )
                ):
                    selected_index = index
                    selected_value = candidate_value
                    selected_delta = delta
            if selected_index < 0 or selected_value is None:
                break
            spins[selected_index] = -spins[selected_index]
            current += selected_delta
            if (step & 127) == 127:
                current = _objective(problem, spins)
            jitter = rng.randrange(max(1, tenure_base // 2 + 1))
            tabu_until[selected_index] = step + tenure_base + jitter
            if _better(problem, current, restart_best_value):
                restart_best_value = current
                restart_best_spins = list(spins)
            if global_best_value is None or _better(problem, current, global_best_value):
                global_best_value = current
                global_best_spins = list(spins)
                best_restart = restart
        exact_restart = _objective(problem, restart_best_spins)
        if global_best_value is None or _better(problem, exact_restart, global_best_value):
            global_best_value = exact_restart
            global_best_spins = restart_best_spins
            best_restart = restart
    assert global_best_spins is not None
    runtime = perf_counter() - start_time
    return _result(
        "tabu",
        problem,
        global_best_spins,
        runtime,
        restarts,
        {
            "backend": "BuiltInTabuSearch",
            "iterations": iterations,
            "restarts": restarts,
            "tabu_tenure": tenure_base,
            "best_restart": best_restart,
            "seed": int(seed) & 0xFFFFFFFF,
        },
    )
def solve_greedy(
    problem: Problem,
    iterations: int,
    restarts: int,
    seed: int,
) -> SoftwareResult:
    start_time = perf_counter()
    iterations = max(1, int(iterations))
    effective_restarts = max(4, int(restarts))
    global_best_spins: List[int] | None = None
    global_best_value: float | None = None
    best_restart = 0
    total_moves = 0
    for restart in range(effective_restarts):
        rng = random.Random((int(seed) + restart * 104729) & 0xFFFFFFFF)
        spins = _random_spins(rng, problem.n)
        current = _objective(problem, spins)
        for _step in range(iterations):
            selected_index = -1
            selected_delta = 0.0
            selected_gain = 0.0
            for index in range(problem.n):
                delta = _flip_delta(problem, spins, index)
                gain = _improvement(problem, delta)
                if gain > selected_gain + 1e-12:
                    selected_gain = gain
                    selected_delta = delta
                    selected_index = index
            if selected_index < 0:
                break
            spins[selected_index] = -spins[selected_index]
            current += selected_delta
            total_moves += 1
        exact_value = _objective(problem, spins)
        if global_best_value is None or _better(problem, exact_value, global_best_value):
            global_best_value = exact_value
            global_best_spins = list(spins)
            best_restart = restart
    assert global_best_spins is not None
    runtime = perf_counter() - start_time
    return _result(
        "greedy",
        problem,
        global_best_spins,
        runtime,
        effective_restarts,
        {
            "backend": "BuiltInMultiStartGreedy",
            "iterations_limit": iterations,
            "restarts": effective_restarts,
            "accepted_moves": total_moves,
            "best_restart": best_restart,
            "seed": int(seed) & 0xFFFFFFFF,
        },
    )
def _ranking_key(problem: Problem, result: SoftwareResult):
    return -result.objective if _maximize(problem) else result.objective
def solve_with_optimizers(
    problem: Problem,
    selection: str = "all",
    iterations: int = 2000,
    restarts: int = 4,
    seed: int = 0x12802026,
) -> SoftwareResult:
    selection = str(selection).strip().lower()
    allowed = {"all", "kaiwu_sa", "local_sa", "tabu", "greedy"}
    if selection not in allowed:
        raise ValueError(f"未知软件优化器选择: {selection}")
    requested = (
        ["kaiwu_sa", "local_sa", "tabu", "greedy"]
        if selection == "all"
        else [selection]
    )
    total_start = perf_counter()
    results: List[SoftwareResult] = []
    errors: Dict[str, str] = {}
    for optimizer_name in requested:
        try:
            if optimizer_name == "kaiwu_sa":
                from kaiwu_bridge import solve_with_kaiwu
                kaiwu_result = solve_with_kaiwu(problem, "sa")
                results.append(
                    SoftwareResult(
                        model="kaiwu_sa",
                        objective=float(kaiwu_result.objective),
                        spins=list(kaiwu_result.spins),
                        binary=list(kaiwu_result.binary),
                        solver_runtime_s=float(kaiwu_result.solver_runtime_s),
                        total_runtime_s=float(kaiwu_result.total_runtime_s),
                        candidate_count=int(kaiwu_result.candidate_count),
                        details=dict(kaiwu_result.details),
                    )
                )
            elif optimizer_name == "local_sa":
                results.append(
                    solve_local_sa(problem, iterations, restarts, seed)
                )
            elif optimizer_name == "tabu":
                results.append(
                    solve_tabu(problem, iterations, restarts, seed ^ 0x13579BDF)
                )
            elif optimizer_name == "greedy":
                results.append(
                    solve_greedy(problem, iterations, restarts, seed ^ 0x2468ACE0)
                )
        except Exception as exc:
            errors[optimizer_name] = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
            if selection != "all":
                raise
    if not results:
        detail = "；".join(f"{key}={value}" for key, value in errors.items())
        raise RuntimeError("所有软件优化器均失败：" + detail)
    for result in results:
        exact = _objective(problem, result.spins)
        if abs(exact - result.objective) > 1e-7:
            result.details["reported_objective_before_recalc"] = result.objective
            result.objective = exact
    results.sort(key=lambda item: _ranking_key(problem, item))
    best = results[0]
    elapsed = perf_counter() - total_start
    ranking = [
        {
            "rank": index + 1,
            "optimizer": result.model,
            "objective": float(result.objective),
            "solver_ms": float(result.solver_runtime_s * 1000.0),
            "total_ms": float(result.total_runtime_s * 1000.0),
            "candidates": int(result.candidate_count),
        }
        for index, result in enumerate(results)
    ]
    merged_details: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "backend": "MultiOptimizerSuite",
        "selected_suite": selection,
        "best_optimizer": best.model,
        "best_solver_runtime_s": float(best.solver_runtime_s),
        "iterations": int(iterations),
        "restarts": int(restarts),
        "seed": int(seed) & 0xFFFFFFFF,
        "ranking": ranking,
        "errors": errors,
        "best_details": best.details,
    }
    value_convention = best.details.get("value_convention")
    if value_convention:
        merged_details["value_convention"] = value_convention
    return SoftwareResult(
        model=best.model,
        objective=float(best.objective),
        spins=list(best.spins),
        binary=list(best.binary),
        solver_runtime_s=float(elapsed),
        total_runtime_s=float(elapsed),
        candidate_count=sum(item.candidate_count for item in results),
        details=merged_details,
    )
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass
from collections import deque
import binascii
import ipaddress
import math
import os
import socket
import subprocess
import struct
import threading
import time
from typing import Iterable, List, Optional
try:
    from . import native_fastpath as _native_fastpath
except Exception:
    try:
        import native_fastpath as _native_fastpath
    except Exception:
        _native_fastpath = None
SOF = b"\x55\xAA"
VERSION = 4
MAX_PAYLOAD = 140000
CMD_PING = 0x01
CMD_LOAD_ISING = 0x10
CMD_LOAD_H_ONLY = 0x11
CMD_LOAD_START = 0x13
CMD_LOAD_H_START = 0x14
CMD_START = 0x20
CMD_STATUS = 0x21
CMD_ABORT = 0x22
CMD_GET_RESULT = 0x23
CMD_SOFT_RESET = 0x24
CMD_UAV_LOAD_START = 0x30
CMD_UAV_H_START = 0x31
CMD_ERROR = 0xFF
FORMAT_Q9_22 = 1
FORMAT_BFP16_J = 2
FORMAT_BFP8_J = 3
FORMAT_UAV_BFP8_J = 4
FORMAT_UAV_BFP16_J = 5
FORMAT_UAV_Q9_22 = 6
FORMAT_UAV_SPARSE_BFP16_J = 7
FLAG_UPPER_TRIANGLE = 1
ERROR_TEXT = {
    1: "协议版本不匹配",
    2: "帧长度错误",
    3: "CRC16校验错误",
    4: "命令不支持",
    5: "问题格式错误",
    6: "问题尚未配置",
    7: "求解器忙",
    8: "求解超时",
    9: "FPGA系数配置失败",
    10: "缓冲区不足",
    11: "PL/AXI求解器不可访问，请检查bitstream与XSA",
}
class FpgaApplicationError(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, code: int, command: int = 0, reported_length: int | None = None):
        self.code = int(code) & 0xFF
        self.command = int(command) & 0xFF
        self.reported_length = reported_length
        text = ERROR_TEXT.get(self.code, f"FPGA错误{self.code}")
        detail = f"FPGA返回错误: {text} (code={self.code}, cmd=0x{self.command:02X}"
        if reported_length is not None:
            detail += f", payload={reported_length} B"
        detail += ")"
        super().__init__(detail)
def _raise_fpga_error(payload: bytes) -> None:
    command = payload[0] if len(payload) > 0 else 0
    code = payload[1] if len(payload) > 1 else 0
    reported_length = struct.unpack_from("<I", payload, 2)[0] if len(payload) >= 6 else None
    raise FpgaApplicationError(code, command, reported_length)
def crc16_ccitt(data: bytes, initial: int = 0xFFFF) -> int:
    return binascii.crc_hqx(data, initial) & 0xFFFF
def build_frame(
    cmd: int,
    seq: int,
    payload: bytes = b"",
    *,
    trusted_udp: bool = False,
) -> bytes:
    if len(payload) > MAX_PAYLOAD:
        raise ValueError(f"payload过大: {len(payload)}")
    header = SOF + bytes((VERSION, cmd & 0xFF)) + struct.pack(
        "<HI", seq & 0xFFFF, len(payload)
    )
    body = header + payload
    crc = 0 if trusted_udp else crc16_ccitt(body)
    return body + struct.pack("<H", crc)
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Frame:
    version: int
    cmd: int
    seq: int
    payload: bytes
    raw: bytes
def parse_frame(raw: bytes) -> Frame:
    if len(raw) < 12 or raw[:2] != SOF:
        raise ValueError("非法帧头/长度")
    version, cmd = raw[2], raw[3]
    seq, length = struct.unpack_from("<HI", raw, 4)
    if len(raw) != 12 + length:
        raise ValueError("帧总长度不匹配")
    expected_crc = struct.unpack_from("<H", raw, len(raw) - 2)[0]
    if crc16_ccitt(raw[:-2]) != expected_crc:
        raise ValueError("CRC16错误")
    return Frame(version, cmd, seq, raw[10:-2], raw)
class FrameParser:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.buffer = bytearray()
    def feed(self, data: bytes) -> List[Frame]:
        self.buffer.extend(data)
        frames: List[Frame] = []
        while True:
            start = self.buffer.find(SOF)
            if start < 0:
                if len(self.buffer) > 1:
                    del self.buffer[:-1]
                break
            if start:
                del self.buffer[:start]
            if len(self.buffer) < 12:
                break
            length = struct.unpack_from("<I", self.buffer, 6)[0]
            total = 12 + length
            if length > MAX_PAYLOAD:
                del self.buffer[0]
                continue
            if len(self.buffer) < total:
                break
            raw = bytes(self.buffer[:total])
            del self.buffer[:total]
            try:
                frames.append(parse_frame(raw))
            except ValueError:
                continue
        return frames
def hex_bytes(data: Iterable[int] | bytes, limit: int = 128) -> str:
    raw = bytes(data)
    text = " ".join(f"{value:02X}" for value in raw[:limit])
    return text + (f" ... ({len(raw)} bytes)" if len(raw) > limit else "")
UDP_MAGIC = b"Q128"
UDP_VERSION = 1
UDP_REQUEST = 1
UDP_RESPONSE = 2
UDP_FAST_REQUEST = 3
UDP_DATA = 1280
UDP_HEADER = struct.Struct("<4sBBIHHHII")
DEFAULT_FPGA_IP = os.environ.get("FPGA_IP", "192.168.2.10")
DEFAULT_HOST_IP = os.environ.get("FPGA_BIND_IP", "192.168.2.100")
DEFAULT_UDP_PORT = int(os.environ.get("FPGA_PORT", "4000"))
def _udp_packet(
    flag: int,
    session: int,
    index: int,
    count: int,
    total: int,
    payload: bytes,
) -> bytes:
    crc = 0 if flag == UDP_FAST_REQUEST else (binascii.crc32(payload) & 0xFFFFFFFF)
    return (
        UDP_HEADER.pack(
            UDP_MAGIC,
            UDP_VERSION,
            flag,
            session,
            index,
            count,
            len(payload),
            total,
            crc,
        )
        + payload
    )
def _udp_unpack(data: bytes):
    if len(data) < UDP_HEADER.size:
        raise ValueError("UDP短包")
    magic, version, flag, session, index, count, dlen, total, crc = UDP_HEADER.unpack_from(data)
    payload = data[UDP_HEADER.size :]
    if (
        magic != UDP_MAGIC
        or version != UDP_VERSION
        or flag not in (UDP_REQUEST, UDP_RESPONSE, UDP_FAST_REQUEST)
        or count == 0
        or count > 128
        or index >= count
        or dlen > UDP_DATA
        or total > MAX_PAYLOAD + 12
        or index * UDP_DATA + dlen > total
        or len(payload) != dlen
        or (
            flag != UDP_FAST_REQUEST
            and (binascii.crc32(payload) & 0xFFFFFFFF) != crc
        )
    ):
        raise ValueError("UDP包头/CRC错误")
    return flag, session, index, count, total, payload
def _broadcast_address(bind_ip: str) -> Optional[str]:
    try:
        ip = ipaddress.IPv4Address(bind_ip)
    except ipaddress.AddressValueError:
        return None
    if ip.is_loopback or ip.is_unspecified:
        return None
    return str(ipaddress.IPv4Network(f"{bind_ip}/24", strict=False).broadcast_address)
def _windows_arp_hint(fpga_ip: str) -> str:
    if os.name != "nt":
        return ""
    try:
        completed = subprocess.run(
            ["arp", "-a"],
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            errors="replace",
            timeout=1.5,
            creationflags=getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0),
        )
        table = completed.stdout or ""
    except Exception:
        return ""
    if fpga_ip in table:
        return f"；ARP表中存在 {fpga_ip}，应继续检查板端UDP 4000和协议响应"
    return (
        f"；ARP表中不存在 {fpga_ip}，板端尚未响应ARP，"
        "优先检查双通道ELF是否已Resume、Platform/XSA和GEM0初始化"
    )
class UdpSession:
    last_transport = "UDP"
    def __init__(
        self,
        fpga_ip: str = DEFAULT_FPGA_IP,
        port: int = DEFAULT_UDP_PORT,
        bind_ip: str = DEFAULT_HOST_IP,
        broadcast_fallback: bool = True,
        spin_wait_us: int = 1500,
        adaptive_spin: bool = False,
        spin_wait_min_us: int = 1500,
        blocking_poll_us: int = 1000,
        incoming_cpu: int = -1,
        kernel_busy_poll_us: int = 0,
        native_receive: bool = False,
    ):
        self.addr = (fpga_ip, int(port))
        self.bind_ip = bind_ip
        self.broadcast_fallback = bool(broadcast_fallback)
        self.seq = 0
        self.lock = threading.Lock()
        self.last_attempts: list[str] = []
        self._sock: Optional[socket.socket] = None
        self._connected_udp = False
        self._io_timeout_s = 0.005
        self.blocking_poll_us = max(250, min(5000, int(blocking_poll_us)))
        self._connected_blocking_poll_s = self.blocking_poll_us * 1.0e-6
        self.incoming_cpu = int(incoming_cpu)
        self.kernel_busy_poll_us = max(0, min(500, int(kernel_busy_poll_us)))
        self.incoming_cpu_applied = False
        self.kernel_busy_poll_applied = False
        self.native_receive_requested = bool(native_receive)
        self.native_receive_applied = False
        self.native_receive_version = 0
        self.native_receive_fallbacks = 0
        self.spin_wait_us = max(0, min(5000, int(spin_wait_us)))
        self.adaptive_spin = bool(adaptive_spin)
        self.spin_wait_min_us = max(0, min(self.spin_wait_us, int(spin_wait_min_us)))
        self._rtt_history_us: deque[float] = deque(maxlen=64)
        self.last_spin_hit = False
        self.last_spin_elapsed_us = 0.0
        self.last_spin_budget_us = float(self.spin_wait_us)
        self.last_receive_phase = "none"
    def _effective_spin_wait_us(self) -> int:
        cap = int(self.spin_wait_us)
        if cap <= 0:
            return 0
        if not self.adaptive_spin or len(self._rtt_history_us) < 8:
            return cap
        ordered = sorted(self._rtt_history_us)
        index = int(math.ceil(0.95 * (len(ordered) - 1)))
        target = int(math.ceil(ordered[index] + 150.0))
        return max(int(self.spin_wait_min_us), min(cap, target))
    def _record_rtt_us(self, elapsed_us: float) -> None:
        if math.isfinite(float(elapsed_us)) and elapsed_us > 0.0:
            self._rtt_history_us.append(float(elapsed_us))
    @property
    def is_open(self) -> bool:
        return True
    def close(self) -> None:
        sock, self._sock = self._sock, None
        self._connected_udp = False
        if sock is not None:
            try:
                sock.close()
            except OSError:
                pass
    def _socket(self) -> socket.socket:
        if self._sock is not None:
            return self._sock
        sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
        sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_RCVBUF, 1 << 20)
        sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_SNDBUF, 1 << 20)
        sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_BROADCAST, 1)
        try:
            sock.setsockopt(socket.IPPROTO_IP, socket.IP_TOS, 0x10)
        except OSError:
            pass
        if self.incoming_cpu >= 0:
            try:
                so_incoming_cpu = getattr(socket, "SO_INCOMING_CPU", 49)
                sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, so_incoming_cpu, int(self.incoming_cpu))
                self.incoming_cpu_applied = True
            except OSError:
                self.incoming_cpu_applied = False
        if self.kernel_busy_poll_us > 0:
            try:
                so_busy_poll = getattr(socket, "SO_BUSY_POLL", 46)
                sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, so_busy_poll, int(self.kernel_busy_poll_us))
                self.kernel_busy_poll_applied = True
            except OSError:
                self.kernel_busy_poll_applied = False
        try:
            sock.bind((self.bind_ip, 0))
        except OSError as exc:
            sock.close()
            raise OSError(
                f"上位机无法绑定本地IP {self.bind_ip}；"
                "请确认有线网卡已配置该地址且处于启用状态"
            ) from exc
        broadcast = _broadcast_address(self.bind_ip) if self.broadcast_fallback else None
        self._connected_udp = False
        if not broadcast:
            try:
                sock.connect(self.addr)
                self._connected_udp = True
            except OSError:
                self._connected_udp = False
        sock.settimeout(self._io_timeout_s)
        self.native_receive_applied = False
        self.native_receive_version = 0
        if self.native_receive_requested and self._connected_udp and _native_fastpath is not None:
            try:
                st = _native_fastpath.status()
                self.native_receive_version = int(st.get("version", 0) or 0)
                self.native_receive_applied = bool(
                    st.get("available", False) and self.native_receive_version >= 2701
                )
            except Exception:
                self.native_receive_applied = False
        self._sock = sock
        return sock
    def _destinations(self, retries: int) -> list[tuple[str, int, str]]:
        count = max(1, int(retries))
        destinations: list[tuple[str, int, str]] = [
            (self.addr[0], self.addr[1], f"unicast#{attempt + 1}")
            for attempt in range(count)
        ]
        if self.broadcast_fallback:
            broadcast = _broadcast_address(self.bind_ip)
            if broadcast and broadcast != self.addr[0]:
                destinations.append((broadcast, self.addr[1], "broadcast"))
        return destinations
    def transact(
        self,
        cmd: int,
        payload: bytes = b"",
        timeout: float = 2.0,
        retries: int = 1,
    ) -> tuple[bytes, float]:
        with self.lock:
            self.seq = (self.seq + 1) & 0xFFFF
            seq = self.seq
            request_flag = (
                UDP_FAST_REQUEST
                if cmd in (
                    CMD_LOAD_ISING,
                    CMD_LOAD_H_ONLY,
                    CMD_LOAD_START,
                    CMD_LOAD_H_START,
                    CMD_UAV_LOAD_START,
                    CMD_UAV_H_START,
                )
                else UDP_REQUEST
            )
            frame = build_frame(
                cmd,
                seq,
                payload,
                trusted_udp=(request_flag == UDP_FAST_REQUEST),
            )
            session = (int(time.time_ns()) ^ seq) & 0xFFFFFFFF
            count = math.ceil(len(frame) / UDP_DATA)
            errors: list[str] = []
            self.last_attempts = []
            self.last_spin_hit = False
            self.last_spin_elapsed_us = 0.0
            self.last_spin_budget_us = float(self._effective_spin_wait_us())
            self.last_receive_phase = "none"
            for dest_ip, dest_port, label in self._destinations(retries):
                self.last_attempts.append(label)
                sock = self._socket()
                try:
                    started = time.perf_counter()
                    for index in range(count):
                        begin = index * UDP_DATA
                        part = frame[begin : begin + UDP_DATA]
                        packet = _udp_packet(
                            request_flag,
                            session,
                            index,
                            count,
                            len(frame),
                            part,
                        )
                        if self._connected_udp and (dest_ip, dest_port) == self.addr:
                            sock.send(packet)
                        else:
                            sock.sendto(packet, (dest_ip, dest_port))
                    chunks: dict[int, bytes] = {}
                    total: Optional[int] = None
                    expected: Optional[int] = None
                    deadline = started + timeout
                    def consume_datagram(data: bytes, source: tuple[str, int]) -> Optional[bytes]:
                        nonlocal total, expected
                        if source[0] != self.addr[0]:
                            return None
                        try:
                            flag, response_session, index, response_count, response_total, part = _udp_unpack(data)
                        except ValueError:
                            return None
                        if flag != UDP_RESPONSE or response_session != session:
                            return None
                        if response_count == 1 and index == 0:
                            raw = part[:response_total]
                        else:
                            chunks[index] = part
                            total = response_total
                            expected = response_count
